Like I said, the men at the domino table, they say things that feel meant for me. And sometimes the radio announcer too, like he’s putting a warning in there for me. My wife says it’s just regular talk, but I feel like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like things people say on the TV, radio, or in public are directed at you or have special meanings that others don't seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor. That’s what I mean. When I’m around the men playing dominoes, some things they say feel like they’re really meant for me. And the radio announcer too, sometimes it sounds like there’s a little warning in there for me. My wife says it’s just regular talk, but I don’t think so. I feel like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel that thoughts are being put into your mind, or that things happening around you have special meanings that are directly related to you, even if others don’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: I don’t feel like thoughts are being put in my mind, no… but things around me do feel like they have meaning for me. My wife says it’s just talk, but I feel like they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel that events, objects, or people's actions have a special meaning or connection to you that others don't seem to understand or believe?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor… that’s what’s been happening. Like with the men at the domino table, some of what they say feels like it’s meant for me. And sometimes the radio announcer too, like he’s giving me a little warning. My wife says it’s just talk, but to me it feels like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe these messages are happening because someone is specifically trying to communicate with you, or do you feel like these things are just meant for you without someone intentionally sending them?\n\nPatient: I feel like they’re meant for me, doctor. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer… it’s like little messages, warnings for me to be careful. I don’t know if they’d admit they’re trying to send them, but I know what I’m hearing. My wife says it’s just talk, but to me it’s not just talk.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these messages are part of a larger plan or plot that others are involved in, or do you think it's just things that you alone need to pay attention to?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s a whole big plan, doctor. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that the people sending you these messages are working together or coordinating in some way to warn you, or is it more that you just pick up on things meant for you without them necessarily knowing they’re sending them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if they’re all working together, doctor. My wife tells me it’s just talk, but I know they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that people you don’t know are sending you special messages through the TV, radio, or other means?\n\nPatient: Yes… I do. Like I said, the men at the domino table, they say things that feel meant for me. And sometimes the radio announcer too, like he’s putting a warning in there for me. My wife says it’s just regular talk, but I feel like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like things people say on the TV, radio, or in public are directed at you or have special meanings that others don't seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor. That’s what I mean. When I’m around the men playing dominoes, some things they say feel like they’re really meant for me. And the radio announcer too, sometimes it sounds like there’s a little warning in there for me. My wife says it’s just regular talk, but I don’t think so. I feel like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel that thoughts are being put into your mind, or that things happening around you have special meanings that are directly related to you, even if others don’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: I don’t feel like thoughts are being put in my mind, no… but things around me do feel like they have meaning for me. Like the men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer, they say things that seem meant for me. My wife says it’s just talk, but I feel like they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel that events, objects, or people's actions have a special meaning or connection to you that others don't seem to understand or believe?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor… that’s what’s been happening. Like with the men at the domino table, some of what they say feels like it’s meant for me. And sometimes the radio announcer too, like he’s giving me a little warning. My wife says it’s just talk, but to me it feels like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe these messages are happening because someone is specifically trying to communicate with you, or do you feel like these things are just meant for you without someone intentionally sending them?\n\nPatient: I feel like they’re meant for me, doctor. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer… it’s like little messages, warnings for me to be careful. I don’t know if they’d admit they’re trying to send them, but I know what I’m hearing. My wife says it’s just talk, but to me it’s not just talk.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these messages are part of a larger plan or plot that others are involved in, or do you think it's just things that you alone need to pay attention to?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s a whole big plan, doctor. It feels more like I need to pay attention. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer, they say little things that are meant for me. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that the people sending you these messages are working together or coordinating in some way to warn you, or is it more that you just pick up on things meant for you without them necessarily knowing they’re sending them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if they’re all working together, doctor. My wife says it’s just talk, but to me it feels like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these messages you're picking up on are happening randomly, or do they seem to follow a pattern that makes sense to you over time?\n\nPatient: It feels like it just happens when it happens, doctor. My wife says it’s only talk, but to me it feels like a warning to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages you're hearing are part of a special system or ability that only you can understand, or do you think anyone could notice them if they paid attention?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s a special system, doctor. I just know when I hear it, like with the men at the domino table or sometimes the radio, it feels meant for me. Maybe someone else could notice if they paid attention, but my wife says it’s just talk. To me, it feels like a warning to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these messages are meant only for you in a way that no one else could understand, even if they listened closely?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor… it feels like they’re meant for me. I don’t know that anybody else would understand it the way I do, even if they heard the same thing. That’s what I mean. When I’m around the men playing dominoes, some things they say feel like they’re really meant for me. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that the people sending you these messages are working together or coordinating in some way to warn you, or is it more that you just pick up on things meant for you without them necessarily knowing they’re sending them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if they’re all working together, doctor. It’s more like I pick up on it when it happens. The men at the domino table say things, and sometimes the radio announcer too, and it feels meant for me. My wife says it’s just regular talk, but I feel like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like things people say on the TV, radio, or in public are directed at you or have special meanings that others don't seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor. That’s what I mean. When I’m around the men playing dominoes, some things they say feel like they’re really meant for me. Like with the men at the domino table, some of what they say feels like it’s meant for me. And sometimes the radio announcer too, like he’s giving me a little warning. My wife says it’s just talk, but to me it feels like they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe these messages are happening because someone is specifically trying to communicate with you, or do you feel like these things are just meant for you without someone intentionally sending them?\n\nPatient: I feel like they’re meant for me, doctor. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer… it’s like little messages, warnings for me to be careful. I don’t know if they’d admit they’re trying to send them, but I know what I’m hearing. My wife says it’s just talk, but to me it’s not just talk.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these messages are part of a larger plan or plot that others are involved in, or do you think it's just things that you alone need to pay attention to?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s a whole big plan, doctor. It feels more like I need to pay attention. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer, they say little things that are meant for me. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that the people sending you these messages are working together or coordinating in some way to warn you, or is it more that you just pick up on things meant for you without them necessarily knowing they’re sending them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if they’re all working together, doctor. It’s more like I pick up on it when it happens. The men at the domino table say things, and sometimes the radio announcer too, and it feels meant for me. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like even random things people do or say—like strangers talking or actions in public—are somehow meant for you or have a direct message for you, even if they don’t realize it?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor… sometimes it feels that way. My wife tells me it’s just talk, but I know what it feels like.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages you're picking up on are part of a consistent pattern or system that you’ve come to understand over time, or do they just seem like separate warnings that come up when you hear them?\n\nPatient: It feels more like separate warnings when they come up, doctor. I don’t have a whole pattern figured out. But when the men at the domino table say certain things, or the radio announcer says something, I can tell it’s meant for me. My wife says it’s only talk, but to me it feels like a warning to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages you're hearing are part of a special system or ability that only you can understand, or do you think anyone could notice them if they paid attention?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s a special system, doctor. I just know when I hear it, like with the men at the domino table or sometimes the radio, it feels meant for me. To me, it feels like a warning to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these messages are meant only for you in a way that no one else could understand, even if they listened closely?\n\nPatient: Yes, doctor… it feels like they’re meant for me. That’s what it feels like. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer, they say little things… and I feel like it’s meant for me, not everybody else. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the men at the domino table or the radio announcer are part of a group that is working against you or trying to harm you in some way?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s a whole group, doctor. That’s how it feels. Other people might hear the same words, but to me it has a meaning for me.
